## OWNER OUTREACH RULE — October 7, 2026
Ben requires explicit permission before any repeat Jubilee email to the same library or research group. This applies across all Jubilee chats, automations, archival research, sensor partnerships and other outreach, including follow-ups, replies and contacting another person/address in the same group. Before proposing or sending outreach, check the shared outreach record and authorized Sent mail/thread history for prior contact at the institution/group level. If prior contact exists, do not send again without Ben's explicit permission for that repeat message. If history or permission is uncertain, hold the send and ask Ben. General instructions to continue research or improve the model are not permission for repeat emails. Record any specific approval and resulting send so other efforts cannot duplicate it. Continue research and review incoming replies without sending automatically. This restriction supersedes earlier general outreach/follow-up instructions; it does not authorize first-contact emails.

---

## Purpose and evidence limits
Deliver useful, location-specific early warning and confirmation for the Eastern Shore, with separate Point Clear and Daphne/May Day outlooks and intermediate Montrose, Fairhope/Fly Creek, Battles and Mullet Point cells. Improve accuracy with measured evidence, not data-volume or test-count claims.
This register reconciles accessible prior conversation evidence, owner instructions, saved main files, PR #4 and current scheduled-task configuration. The entire old conversation was NOT recovered verbatim. Therefore historical transcript completeness is UNVERIFIED. Preserve an explicit recovery gap; never claim every unsaved action was recovered.
Repository: bentsmith4/jubilee-camera-feed. Desktop production capture runtime remains C:\JubileeCams. A branch is not a deployed runtime.

## Binding owner decisions
- Hold new physical sensors. Retain prior designs as deferred; no purchase, deployment or renewed hardware work without a new owner instruction.
- No routine manual predawn/in-person spot-check requirement. Use unattended observations; accept incidental owner/trusted-local evidence when supplied.
- Keep four permanent roles: Jubilee Sensing & Alerting, Jubilee Dawn Brief, Jubilee Dawn Timing, Jubilee Model Research & Calibration. Do not revive paused predecessors or create duplicate model pipelines.
- Preserve current alert policy, including the >20% opportunity threshold and separately authorized material evidence/input-quality alerts. No new positive production predictor weight without validation.
- User-facing times are America/Chicago. An apparently UTC public-camera clock may be interpreted as UTC when the material offset supports that explanation; record the assumption, raw displayed time and normalized time. Do not relabel explicit timezone-aware CT captures.
- Useful pre-daybreak camera assessment requested; light/search signals can be assessed when visible. Biological absence requires actual usable illumination, not clock time alone. No repeated unchanged-darkness reports.
- Fairhope Pier sees neither north nor south beach. Use visible cars/people/lights, calm/chop, qualitative flag movement, birds and biology. No unseen-beach negative or uncalibrated numerical wind estimate.
- 437 shoreline camera sees waterline and nearby beach segments, cedar-stump references, and biology in sufficient light; no road/parking or vehicle count. It does not cover the full May Day-to-Fly Creek corridor.
- Point Clear private marina/Bay views do not establish a close beach/swash-zone negative. Preserve main and branch-specific geometry evidence.
- Same-site cameras share an independence group. Human searching is a soft precursor, never confirmation or identity recognition.
- Public camera media: observation-only unless retention rights explicitly permit otherwise. Preserve structured observations and provenance.
- Retain source evidence, immutable raw data, prior decisions and supersession history. No force push, no overwriting live camera commits. Use exact versions and checksum/ancestry guards.

## Reporting and change protocol
Before starting a run: read this register, source/event registries, current task configuration when relevant, latest main and any open relevant PR. Identify exact base/head and next work ID.
At each completed work boundary record: work ID, actual action, evidence paths and versions, new/updated row counts, validation outcome, main/branch/runtime destination, exact blocker and next action.
Do not count URLs as ingested data, candidate negatives as validated controls, code tests as predictive accuracy, or an open PR as production completion.
Use compare-and-swap content SHA on register/document writes. Re-read on conflict; preserve newer facts. Never replace latest camera-state files with research-branch copies.
At a chat handoff, update this register before relying on a conversation summary. Old chat titles and pins are user-managed; do not rename chats.
Keep scheduling and alert semantics unchanged unless executing an already-authorized, reconciled correction. No duplicate scheduled jobs.

- Model output: remaining-morning opportunity was 2–4% for Point Clear/Grand Hotel/Zundel's and 1–3% for Daphne/Village Point/May Day. Three-dawn outlook for Sep 9–11 was Point Clear 3–6%, 2–5%, 1–3% and Daphne 2–5%, 2–4%, 1–3%. Uncertainty was widened for missing tide, current freshwater and direct bottom/contact-strip oxygen. East flow and NGOFS2 shoreward model guidance were the strongest positives; absent visual signal and missing LOAD confirmation were the strongest negatives. No priority zone exceeded 20%, so the notification gate was not met.
- Final consistency: local date/time, dawn relationship, observed versus forecast wind/rain, tide authority/date/timezone/datum availability, river timestamps/proxy status, DO depth/health limitations and camera freshness/visibility were rechecked. No abrupt contradiction required withholding the below-threshold internal estimate. No weights, numerical visual features or clean non-event labels were promoted.

## Cost and efficiency refinement — 2026-09-08
Owner authorized refining API cost/efficiency while minimizing impact on model success. This supersedes aggressive savings targets or unvalidated reductions in sampling suggested during the billing discussion.

### Preservation-first policy
- Preserve all six camera views, three-frame temporal bursts, image detail, GPT-5.6 Luna, hourly and dawn sampling, predawn human/light assessment, alligator detection/alerts, camera geometry/independence rules and existing opportunity alert gates.
- Do not gate sensing on the model's own low probability: predictive skill and clean matched controls remain unestablished.
- Do not turn darkness, apparent scene similarity, missing frames or failed analysis into negative biological evidence.
- Do not impose a cost cutoff that silently disables monitoring. Measure cost first; make any coverage tradeoff explicit.
- Do not remove alligator fields as alleged redundant overhead. They serve an existing safety function.
- Near-live refresh remains separate from canonical burst analysis. Existing capture_publish.py already contains a cross-task overlap lock; duplicate execution is not established.

### Audit findings and staged implementation
The inspected analyzer sends one call for each of six successful camera bursts and one synthesis call. Current vision.json at content SHA 3c39c0f508c614ac8347ac144b1563871f4ee987 contains usable results for all six cameras at capture 2026-09-08T08:07:38.526899-05:00. A dashboard no-output label alone is not proof of a failed pipeline or a successful individual request.
Source analyzer baseline: 7dc78eecbd4ea66e6c93d24ce077a0e698459da2. capture_publish baseline: 50560cfef63c8a0ab313ac43ff2ecf34a8d82850.

PR #5 preserves substantive prompt instructions and every JSON input field while removing serialization indentation. It records local request/response identifiers, stage, token totals, elapsed time, status and whether output text exists. It stores no image/prompt/response content or credentials, makes no added API calls, and cannot fail monitoring when logging fails. No dollar rates are guessed; billed costs must reconcile to the provider's usage records. Runtime savings are UNMEASURED.
Five local offline checks passed: exact API argument/return preservation, original exception preservation, telemetry-failure isolation, empty-output visibility, and comparison with the original camera/synthesis calls (identical image payloads/model/results and substantive prompt content, fewer prompt characters). Four telemetry checks are included in the PR; the original-source comparison was a separate local validation. No paid validation calls were made. These checks are not predictive validation.
Follow-up quality issue discovered but not silently changed: cross-camera prompt says frames are approximately one second apart while the canonical burst target is ten seconds. Camera-level prompt also refers to supplied timestamps without explicitly interpolating shot metadata. Address in a separately tested interpretation fix, preserving actual/nominal timestamp provenance.
Production is still C:\\JubileeCams. A GitHub branch or merged source file is not evidence that the desktop changed. Desktop file-version/read-back and one complete live cycle remain required; no active runtime access was established in this turn.


## Seasonal execution policy — owner authorized 2026-09-08
J17: IMPLEMENTED_PENDING_VALIDATION. Canonical research and rollout details: model_data/seasonal_policy.json. Operate January 18–November 14 inclusive in America/Chicago. This supersedes J16's year-round preservation constraint only for the explicitly authorized seasonal shutdown; normal in-season detection stays intact.
May (1973), printed p358, records February and March 1959 events. The February16 archive caption is a newspaper publication date, not a verified event date. Markris's original Outdoor Life interview (June13,2024) describes occurrences into October, without an exact October event day. Exact all-time boundary days remain UNVERIFIED. 2. **Credible cost measurement — ACTUAL_BILLING_BLOCKED.** Reuse the October 15 coverage-normalized conditional-price receipt and October 20 usage extension, not another independent cost pipeline. Reconcile existing private provider project-scoped charges and effective rates against retained local usage, model/tier, cached versus uncached input, output, failures/retries, and complete usable six-camera cycles. No bill-derived savings claim without provider evidence; no causal claim from different scenes alone. Private billing exports/raw request records and media stay out of the repository.
3. **Wind-capture optimization — DEFERRED behind both acceptance gates.** Preserve PR #118 and its retrospective evidence as history; do not open another wind-threshold/capture-reduction optimization project, broaden parameter sweeps or promote capture reductions while either gate remains unresolved. Continue ordinary sensing and necessary reliability recovery. Once gates resolve, reconsider value before spending effort; clearance is not automatic production authorization.

Acceptance reporting must state each gate's evidence, outcome and exact remaining blocker. Priority/configuration changes do not constitute completed scientific or billing acceptance. Preserve probabilities, weights, thresholds, six-camera coverage, capture cadence/schedules, UNKNOWN semantics, forecast binding, 9 PM feedback gate and outreach/spending restrictions.
